Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Fri Sep 08 2017 16:27:41 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Proposed SSDMT Act – Sanatan Sampradaya Devalaya Management Trust Act
.
(a) to free Hindu \ Sanatan Devalayas i.e. temples from Govt control ; and
.
(b) to create an SGPC like management structure over Hindu Devalayas , if and only if , the existing trustees \ owners wish to create such a management structure.
.
------------------------------------------
.
[ comment : This SSDMT law-draft will need to be passed both houses of Parliament with a simple majority.
.
This draft has 2 parts -
.
Part - I : Basic instructions for citizens
.
Part - II : Other instructions for Officers, citizens and comments
.
Important clauses are – (1) , (2.2) , (4) , (4.1) , (4.2) , (8) , (8.1) , (9.1) , (9.7) , (9.8) , (9.9) , (9.9.1) , (9.9.2) , (13.1) , (13.3) , (14.4) , (RTR.2)
.
The comments listed are not binding on any Officer / Minister / judges. The Jurors , Citizens and all can use it as guidelines. But they are not binding. ]
.
-----------------------------------
Part - I : Basic instructions for citizens
------------------------------------
.
(1) This law applies only for Devalaya of Sanatan Sampradaya across India, and Trusts (or bodies) which own these Devalayas. The word “Sanatan” in this law includes all followers of all Hindu Sampradayas and other Sanatan Sampradaya \ Panth which originated in Indian subcontinent. The law does not put label of Hindu on those sects or citizens, who do not wish to be called as Hindu. They may apply this law on their Devalaya without applying label of Hindu on themselves and their Sampraday.
.
(1.1) This law will NOT put any restriction on followers and shrines of Islam, Christianity, Zoroasterism and religions which originated outside Indian subcontinent. They will not be covered under this law. And since Sikhs already have SGPC Act, this law will also not apply on Sikh Gurudwara as well.
.
(1.2) comment : This law will initiate a Jury Trials, to decide ownership of all Devalayas under Government control. The Jurors will consist of 12 to 1500 Hindu voters between the age of 30 years and 55 years , chosen at random from the voter list of District / State / India as may be the case. The Jurors will decide which claimant has what % ownership / trusteeship over the Devalaya. And after deciding, will handover the Devalaya to the owners and free it from Govt control. The trial in most cases will be over within 6 months.
.
(1.3) comment : This law will enable the owners / trustees of Devalayas to create an SGPC (SGPC means Sikh Gurudrwara Prabadhank Committee) like structure to manage Devalayas. All voters are requested to read and study SGPC Act to know its details. The owners / trustees of the trust may decide NOT to create such SGPC like structure over their Devalayas. But owners decide to create such SGPC like structure to manage their Devalayas, then the Bhakts (followers) of the Sampraday will elect the heads and Juries will chosen from Bhakts will decide the disputes. And if the owners / trustees decide to create such a structure, then they cannot get old management structure back.
.
(2) PM shall appoint a National Registrar. National Registrar will keep list of owners, trustees and Bhakts (followers) all Devalaya and Hindu \ Sanatan Religious Trusts in India. The National Registrar will appoint State Registrars and District Registrars to manage the task at State \ District Level.
.
(2.1) National Registrar, State Registrar and District Registrar are referred as NR, SR and DR in this act.
.
(2.2) You (you here means a Hindu \ Sanatan voter including Sikh, Jain, Buddhist voter, residing anywhere in India) can replace NR using procedure described in “(RTR) Right to Recall NR” clause given later. NR will print RTR procedures for State / District Registrars.
.
(3) In case there is a dispute between Devalaya Trustees, the DR / SR / NR , will randomly select 12 to 1500 Hindu \ Sanatan voters between age of 30 years and 55 years from District / State or India, to resolve the disputes between Devalaya owners / trustees. And DR / SR / NR will hand over all Devalaya under Govt control back to trustees and owners. The Jurors will also decide the weight of the votes of each owner / trustee, using a procedure given in later clauses.
.
(4) This law will give two options to Devalaya trustees / owners to manage the Devalaya in two ways (4a) the owners or trustees themselves using their votes can appoint / expel Devalaya Managers and staff, who will manage the Devalaya , and disputes will decided by Managers or Trustees / owners (4b) owners / trustees can create a structure in which each Bhakt will be a voting member, and Bhakts elect / recall the Devalaya Chairman, and Jurors selected from Bhakts will decide the disputes amongst staff and Bhakts.
.
(4.1) It will be optional for Devalaya trustees / owners to create such democratic structure. It is not all required at all by this law that trustees / owners must create such a democratic structure. If the trustee / owners want, then they can continue to run the Devalaya on their own, without giving electoral or management powers to the Bhakts.
.
(4.2) But if the trustees / owners decide to form a democratic structure, then they cannot take this control back. So formation of the democratic structure is a “one way street”.
.
-----------------------------------
Part - II : Instructions for Citizens, Officers and comments
------------------------------------

(5) Appointment \ Recalls of Registrars :
.
(5.1) The PM will appoint an officer titled as National Registrar for Sanatan Sampradaya Trusts, henceforth referred as NRSST , or National Registrar or simply NR. NR will be recallable by voters of all Bharatiya Sampraday across India such as Hinduism, all sects with roots in India, Jains, Buddhists, Sikhs etc.
.
(5.1.1) comment : The Right to Recall over National Registrar procedure is detailed in later clauses.
.
(5.1.2) comment : The Sikhs have been visiting Hindu Devalayas over and above Gurudwara since centauries, and have also been giving donations to Devalayas, and have worked to protect the Devalayas in past. And so they will have voting rights in RTR-NR)
.
(5.2) NR will appoint State Registrars (SR) and District Registrars (DR). NR will also print procedures by which Hindu \ Sanatan voters of State can replace SR and Hindu \ Sanatan voters of a District can replace DR. If Hindu voters find these procedures weak, then they may add procedure using (CV) clauses given later or replace NR using RTR-NR clauses given later.
.
(6) Registration of Devalaya, Devalaya owning Trust, and Trustees
.
(6.1) For rest of the act, the word “trustee” will include “owner” if the Devalaya owning body is a private body and not a trust. And the word trust here will include that “private body”, if that private body is owning the Devalaya.
.
(6.2) If Devalaya is directly owned by one or more, but less than 20 persons, then Devalaya owners will be free not to register themselves and not to register their Devalayas with DR. They may or may not register. But if Devalaya is owned by a registered private body or a trust or registered association or any non-person entity, or owned by 20 or more persons, then Devalaya and owning body must register themselves with DR.
.
(6.2.1) They will register within 30 days after DR issue notifications. After that, registration can be there, but Jurors may impose a fine for being late.
.
(6.3) DR will post the name and location of each Devalaya on his website. DR will also post the names, addresses etc of the Trust that owns the Devalaya. DR will issue a serial number for the Devalaya as well as the Trust. DR will also publish the Trust Deed (or partnership deed or document that shows formation of the private body that owns the Devalaya, or simply name of owners if that Devalaya is owned by one or more persons without any body). SR and NR will combine this data across all districts of India and put on their websites.
.
(6.4) DR will post names and Aadhar number of every Trustee, or every person who claims to be Trustee.
.
(6.4.1) If later, Jurors decide that he had knowingly made a false claim that he is Trustee, and he knew that he had no basis to make such claim, then Jurors may imprison him for 3 years.
.
(6.4.2) His claim will be accepted or rejected later using Jury Trial. But DR must register all claims.
.
(6.4.3) If trustee doesn’t have Aadhar number, then DR will use his PAN or Passport Number or Voter Number or number issued by DR or number issued by SR / NR. And DR will also put names / numbers of persons who were trustees in past along with when they were appointed and when they left, as far as records become available.
.
(6.5) SR / NR will combine and put information for State / India.
.
(6.6) For all official work, the Trustee will have to come to the DR or offices designated by DR to manage the Trusts. If the Trustee is living in same district in which the registered office of the Trust is located, then the Trustee will have to visit that DR office. If the Trustee is living in some other District, then the Trustee may visit DR where he lives in or DR where the Trust is registered. DR may work to delegate some work to Talati.
.
(6.7) DR will register all trustees’ claims, that they are trustees, without asking for proofs. Later, if there is dispute, on whether a person who claims to be trustee is not trustee, then Jurors will decide if he is trustee or not. And Jurors will decide the fine for making false claims. But DR will admit all claims and post them on website.
.
(6.8) If a person who claims that he is Trustee of a Trust, must Register his name within 30 days after DR issues notification for Registration. The fee after 30 days will be Rs 1000 higher for delay of each month. No claim will be admitted 10 years after this law is passed..
.
(7) Registration and annual fees
.
(7.1) DR will charge a fee of Rs 2000 one time, and Rs 1000 per year from each Devalaya in the district where Trust is registered. If Trust owns Devalaya in District other than District where Trust is registered, then fees will be twice.
.
(7.2) If the Trustee resides in District other than District where Trust is registered, then DR will charge Rs 2000 one time and Rs 1000 per year. If Trustee resides in District other than District where Trust is registered, then fee will be twice.
.
(7.3) The fee for Chairman will be twice the trustee.
.
(8) Displaying assets owned by Trusts
.
(8.1) Each trustee will submit the list of assets that trust owns, best of his information, to DR. The list will include the locations of the plots it owns, area of constructions, cash, FDs, shares, bonds, gold, silver, furniture, loans given, loans taken and all assets , along with circle rates (aka Jantri) in the area where plot/constructions is located. DR will display all the information on website. One or more trustee may sign a list of asset prepared by one Trustee.
.
(8.2) DR will obtain the circle rates of each property listed in the list and display the information on DR’s website.
.
(9) Resolving disputes of Trusteeship and deciding each Trustee’s weight
.
(9.1) Any Trustee (i.e. person who claims to be Trustee) can prepare a list of persons whom he thinks are Trustees with their Aadhar number or other ID numbers, as per the Trust Deed and agreement of Trustees and subsequent Trust Resolutions etc, best to his information. The Trustee must give this list to DR within 3 months after this law is passed or Jury may levy a late fine. The list must also have weight of each Trustee’s vote, as per Trust deed and / or agreement of Trustees and best to his information.
.
(9.1.1) Votes of Trustees, in Trusts registered under this act, may have same weights or different weights, as decided by Trustee. All trustees must agree. If there is dispute, it will be decided by Jurors
.
(9.2) A trustee can prepare the list himself or approve the list prepared by another Trustee. But each Trustee must prepare one list himself or approve exactly one list. If he doesn’t prepare or approve any list, DR will summon him and remove that Trustee’s name within 30 days.
.
(9.3) The Trustee who is giving list must also specify weights of the votes of the members. And if the weights are specified, then they must add up to 100 or at least 99.99 . The weights must be decimal digits and not fractions , and can have at most 15 digits after decimal. If the weights do not add up to 100, then DR will re-scale the weights so that they add upto 100 or at least 99.99. If weights are not given then DR will post the list, and mark it as “useless”.
.
(9.4) A trustee can submit new list anyday and he can change list he approves anyday. But he cannot modify any list he has submitted.
.
(9.5) The DR will both charge following fees to the Trustee who is submitting list :
.
(a) Less than 10 Trustees - Rs 500 per Trustee
(b) 10 to 100 trustees --- Rs 5000 + Rs 50 per trustee
(c) 10 to 1000 trustees --- Rs 10000 + Rs 20 per trustee
.
The Trustee who is approving a list, and not submitting a new list, will only pay Rs 500. And if a Trustee who has submitted one list, is submitting a new list, then fees will be Rs 5000, plus difference between new fee based on trustee count and previously paid fee, only if the new fee is higher. If a trustee has approved a list, and he wants to approval a different list, then charge will be Rs 1000.
.
(9.6) DR will put each list submitted by Trustees on the website. (a) If the Trustees have presented only one list, and that list is approved by all the Trustees, OR all lists presented by all Trustees are identical wrt Trustees and their weights, then DR will take first submitted list as undisputed list. (b) But if the lists submitted by Trustees have different Trustees and/or have different weights, then DR will call for Jury Trial to decide the final list of Trustees and their weights.
.
(9.7) The DR will call a Jury of Hindu \ Sanatan 15-1500 citizens chosen at random from voter list of the district between the age of 40 years and 55 years to decide --- who are trustees and who are not , and what is the weight of each trustee’s vote. The size of Jury will depend on assets in the Trust, and will be 15 Jurors and 1 Juror more per Rs 1 crore of asset. If any Trustee wants to change the District to District which has largest Devalaya (largest by donation collection in past three years before the law is passed), then he may ask NR. NR will select 5 districts at random, and each DR will call a Jury of exactly 15 Hindu \ Sanatan to decide. If three or over three Jurors agree, then NR will move the case to that District.
.
(9.8) Each Trustee will present Member List with weights before the Jurors. And DR will give his list best as per his opinions and findings. In addition, one Juror may allow any one voter of India to present a list of Trustees for that trust with weights. The Jurors with majority vote will decide which Trustee or which List Preparer will speak for how many minutes.
.
(9.9) After all speeches, each Juror will either (a) approve a list OR (b) he will give 0-100 weight to each person who is claiming to be Trustee. .
.
(9.9.1) The list given by each Juror should be such that weights must add to over 99.99. If a Juror has not give any weight to a Trustee, then it will be presumed as zero weight. If weights don’t add to over 99.99, then DR will rescale the weights so that they add up to over 99.99.
.
(9.9.2) DR will take average of all weights that a Trustee claimant got from all Jurors, and display that as a final list.
.
(9.10) After the list is finalized, each Juror will cite a rupee number to be given to each List Preparer from the Trust. The rupee number must be between Rs 0 and 2% of the Trust Wealth. If the amount is higher than 2% of Trust Wealth, then DR will lower it to 2% of Trust wealth. And the sum of all amounts in the list made by Jurors too should be below 2% of Trust wealth. If the sum is above this limit, then DR will rescale them so that sum is 2% of the wealth. But if the sum is below this limit, then DR will not rescale it.
.
(9.10.1) After this, DR will add the numbers cited by each Juror for each List Preparer. The grand total of all payments given to all List Preparers. Again, all grand total must below 2% of the trust wealth. And if the amount is more, then all totals will be rescaled so that final sum of all sums if below 2% of the trust’s wealth. DR will take that amount from the Trust and will pay that amount to the List Preparers.
.
(9.11) A Juror may also announce a fine between Rs 0 and 2% of wealth of the person who had claimed to be Trustee, if the Juror thinks that that person had willfully prepared or approved a false list. The Juror can also cite a number between 0 months and 36 months of imprisonment, if the Juror thinks that that person had criminal intent in giving such false information. DR will take median fine and median imprisonment as final fine and imprisonment.
.
(9.12) If anyone is not satisfied with the final list of Trustees and weights , then they can appeal before SR. SR will randomly pick three districts in the State and send the complain to DR of those 3 Districts. Each DR will call a Jury of same size or larger size as the size of the Jury called in the first District within 30 days. Each Jurors in each Jury will give 0-100 points to the list presented in the first trial. No new list can be presented in the appeal. SR will declare the list with largest points across 3 Juries as the final list. The Jurors can also announce fines and imprisonment length in months. And SR will impose median fine and median imprisonment sentence.
.
(9.13) If anyone is not satisfied with verdict of SR, then they can appeal before NR. NR will randomly pick five districts across India, in the States other than where Trial was held. in the State and send the complain to DR of those 5 Districts. Each DR will call a Jury of same size or larger size as the size of the Jury called in the first District within 30 days. Each Jurors in each Jury will give 0-100 points to the list presented in the first trial. No new list can be presented in the appeal. NR will declare the list with largest points across 5 Juries as the final list. The Jurors can also announce fines and imprisonment length in months. And NR will impose median fine and median imprisonment sentence.
.
(10) Procedures by which Trustees can appoint / recall Trust Chairman and Trust Committee Members
.
(10.1) Each Trust will have one Chairman and at most 20 committee members. Each Trustee will give number of committee members trust should have to DR. DR will take median number, taking the weights of Trustees into account.
.
(10.2) Appointment and recall of Chairman by Trustees, in Trust with Non-Democratic Structure
.
(10.2.1) Any person who wishes to be Chairman for a Trust can register his name. If he is Trustee, then he doesn’t need any person to second his name. If the person is NOT a Trustee, then he will need a Trustee to second his name. The fees will be Rs 2000 for Chairman candidate and Rs 1000 for Committee member other than Chairman.
.
(10.2.2) A Trustee can give (-100 to 100) Points to a any Chairman candidate. The Points will come on DR’s website and thus it will be open voting. The vote filing fee for trustee will be Rs 50 per score he is giving (so if he is giving scores to say 8 candidates, then fee will be Rs 400). The Trustee can change score anyday. The fee will be Rs 50 per change. (so if Trustee changes Points of say 10 candidates, then fee will be Rs 500). If a Trustee has not given score to any candidate, then it will be taken as -100.
.
(10.2.3) A trustee can transfer his voting rights to a Trustee or non-Trustee for a fee of Rs 500. And he can revoke the voting rights anyday for no fee. If a person has voting rights for more than one Trustee, then he may file same vote for all trustees or file differently. If he is filing differently, then separate filing fees will apply. If he is filing together, then fee will be fee for one, plus Rs 50 for each Trustee.
.
(10.2.4) DR will multiply the points given by a trustee with weight that that Trustee has and add the Points received. If a person has obtained positive points from majority of Trustee, counting weights, then DR will ignore negative Points given to him. The DR will appoint the Chairman candidate who has highest total of weighted Points as the Chairman of the Trust
.
(10.2.5) Every 2 years, the candidate with highest Points will be appointed as Chairman by DR
.
(10.2.6) Recall of Chairman before 2 years by Trustees : any day, if a Chairman candidate as over 33% of highest possible Points, and he has over 2% of the Points that current Chairman has, and the difference stays for more than 30 says, then DR will remove the existing Chairman and replace him with Chairman with highest Points
.
(10.3) Appointment and Recall of Committee Members
.
(10.3.1) Any person who wishes to be Committee Member for a Trust can register his name. Fee will be same as fee for Chairman.
.
(10.3.2) A Trustee can register whom he prefers as Committee Members in order of 1 to as many he wishes. The fee will be Rs 100 per preference he gives. A trustee can change his order of preference anyday. Fee will same as per first filing.
.
(10.3.3) A Trustee can give his voting rights to any Trustee or non Trustee for a fee of Rs 500 and revoke the rights anyday for no fee. If the person has voting rights of another Trustees, then he can vote for all Trustees together or separately. If he is voting together, then fee will be
.
(10.3.4) The votes of a Trustee will be multiplied by the weight of his votes
.
(10.3.5) DR will use system will similar to Rajyasabha members are elected (as in year 2016) with a difference that each vote has a different weight.
.
(a) Say number of committee members to be appointed are N and say sum of all weights is 100. Then a committee member will need X = (100/N + 0.001) or highest votes to become member
.
(b) DR will add first preference votes for each candidate. If one candidate has obtained over X votes, then he will be declared as winner, and the X votes he got will be marked as “consumed”, And the remaining votes will be given to second preference. This step will be repeated, till all votes are consumed, and N candidates are selected.
.
(c) NR will print detailed instructions. The system should be identical to system where Rajyasabha MPs are elected.
.
(d) The voting will be open. All preferences etc cited by Trustees will come on DR’s public website.
.
(10.3.6) DR will change committee members every two years using above procedure.
.
(10.3.6) During those two years, if a candidate’s approval is at least X and 2% higher than approval of the least approved committee member, then DR will remove the least approved committee member and appoint the candidate with highest approval
.
(11) Nominee : A trustee can appoint one or more nominees for his Trusteeship.
.
(11.1) Transfer or sale : A trustee may permanently transfer part or whole of his Trusteeship. A trustee may legally accept money or other consideration for permanent transfer. But a trustee cannot mortgage or give power of Attorney on his Trusteeship, or transfer for a duration, and make it part of any contract.
.
(12) Dissolution of Trust :
.
(12.1) If over 75% Trustees, counted by weights, inform DR (by personally appearing at DR’s officer where Trust is registered) that they want to dissolve the Trust, the DR will conduct the auction of all Trust wealth and distribute the money as per the weights of the Trustees, after paying all taxes, loans, pending salaries of employees etc in that order.
.
(12.2) The amount received by Trustees will be taxed at 1.25 times the highest income tax rate on other normal income of individual (not corporate) . DR will first subtract the auctions fees as decided by NR. Two thirds of the tax received will be equally distributed amongst all Hindu \ Sanatan (including Sikhs, Jains, Buddhists).
.
(12.3) comment : example - so if highest income tax rate is 30%, then tax on the amount received by Trustees will be 1.25 * 30% = 37.5%.
.
(12.4) If 75% of Trustees announce that they want to sell whole or part of the Trusts’ assets, then DR will conduct auctions on those assets and each Trustee will get as per his weights. The tax applicable will be same as above clauses.
.
(13) Transformation to Democratic Structure
.
(13.1) if over 75% of Trustees by weighted votes, agree and cite at DR’s office, that they want “democratic structure” over their Trust, then 90 days after they have applied in person, DR will announce that Hindu / Sanatan Sampradaya Trust as Trust with Democratic Structure. During these 90 days, the Trustees may withdraw the application.
.
(13.2) At the time of application, the Trustees must provide list of all Voting Members and Non Voting Members. The Voting Members must be voters of India or PIO or OCI. The Non Voting Members can be minors and citizens of any country. The list must be approved by over 75% of Trustees. The minors will automatically become Voting Member when they becomes major.
.
(13.3) Once a Trust adopts democratic structure, its voting members will elect the Chairman using open voting via DR’s website. And Chairman will be recallable. The Trust will have 4 to 20 Committee Members depending on the assets of Trust. And 65% Committee Members will be elected by voting members and 35% committee members will be elected as well as non-voting members. The Committee Members elected by Non-Voting Members will not have voting rights inside committee. All committee members will be recallable.
.
(13.4) NR will print the procedure to elect and recall Chairman and Committee Members for the trust via Trusts’ voting members. If citizens of India don’t like that procedure, citizens can replace NR using RTR NR procedure.
.
(13.5) Once a Trust takes Democratic Structure, the officers employed by the Trust can will be first tried by Jurors and then their guilt or innocence and punishment will be decided by the Jurors chosen from the Voting Members. The maximum punishment can be 2 months of salary and expulsion, but no imprisonment, If Trust’s Chairman or Voting Members want more punishment, then they will need to file case against the officer in civil or criminal or other applicable court.
.
(14) Membership in Hindu \ Sanatan Trusts with Democratic Structure
,
(14.1) NR, with help of SR and DR, will maintain all lists of Voting Members and Non-voting Members in all Hindu \ Sanatan Religious Trusts which have opted for Democratic Structure under this act,
.
(14.2) Initial list will be given by Trustees as per clause-13.2, or will be made when the Trust is formed.
,
(14.3) A Voting or Non Voting Member can leave membership anyday. DR will put a public notice and after 6 months, DR will remove his membership. During 6 months, he may cancel his decision to retain Membership
.
(14.4) A Trust can remove Voting Member or Non voting Member only after an internal Jury Trial which will be conducted by DR. DR will select 15 to 1500 Voting Members of the Trust between the age of 25 years and 55 years and ask if the Voting Member should be expelled. The Jurors will hear complainer and the Member. The size of Jury will depend on assets owned by the member and will be minimum 15 and 1 Juror more for every Rs 1 crore.
.
(14.5) If and only if over 75% Jurors approve expulsion, then DR will put his name for expulsion on website. The decision of District Jury can cancelled by State Jury of Trust and decision of State Jury of the Trust can be cancelled by National Jury of the Trust. The decision of National Jury can be cancelled only by Jury of formed by Hindu \ Sanatan voters across State or Hindu \ Sanatan voters across India.
.
(14.6) NR will print the detailed code to run the Jury Trials. The Hindu \ Sanatan voters may replace NR if the code he has printed is weak, using RTR-NR clauses.
.
(14.7) If a person wants to join another Hindu \ Sanatan Religious Trust, with Democratic Structure, registered under this act, then he can submit his application at DR. DR will post his wish on the website. The Trust he wishes to join
.
(14.8) DR will choose 15 to 1500 Voting Members across India, of the Trust at random. The number of Jurors selected will depend on Wealth of the member who is applying. The Jurors needed not physically come to DR office. Each Voting Member will register YES / NO using SMS or AAP or by personally visiting DR office at any District or personally visiting Talati office, within 30 days after application is posted. If below 75% Voting Members register YES, then DR will cancel the application. And if over 75% of Voting Members register YES, then DR will wait for 60 days. During these 60 days, if over 51% of all Voting Members register NO, then DR will cancel application or else, DR will list him as Non-Voting Member in Trust. And DR will repeat same process after 2 years to decide whether he should be made Voting Member or not.
.
(14.9) Son / Daughter of a male or female Voting Member will automatically become a Non-Voting Member at birth, and will become Voting Member when he reaches 18 years of age, unless he decides not to be. If a male Voting Member marries a female who is not a Voting Member, then she will become Voting Member after 2 years. But if a female who is a Voting Member marries a male who is not a Voting Member, then female will loose Membership after 2 years and male will need not automatically get Voting Membership. Both may later apply for Membership.
.
(14.10) A member may lose membership if he converts to any other religion. The Jury selected from the Trust will have final decision
.
(14.11) Membership cannot be sold or transferred by contract or by inheritance
.
(15) Division of a Trust
.
(15.1) If over 25% Trustees or 25% of Voting Members want a division, then any Trustee / Member can submit an Asset Division Proposal. DR will put all Asset Division Proposals on net for a fee decided by NR
.
(15.2) DR (or SR or NR) will summon of Jury of 15 to 1500 Hindu \ Sanatan voters across District or State or India, between the age of 30 years to 55 years. If Trust has over 75% of assets in District, then DR will select all Jurors from District. If no such District is there, but over 75% of assets are in State, then SR will select Jurors from State. And if assets are across India, then NR will choose Jurors from Districts across India. The numbers of Jurors chosen from District will depend on % of assets the Trust has in a given district.
.
(15.3) Each Juror will give (-100 to 100) Points to each Asset Division Proposal. If a proposal has positive Points from over 33% of Jurors, then all its negative Points will be ignored. The proposal with highest Points will be accepted as judgment for Asset Division.
.
(15.4) DR / SR / NR will divide the assets as per the proposal approved by Jurors
.
(15.5) DR / SR / NR will collect expenses from both parties in ratio of assets they got
.
(16) Merger of two Trusts
.
(16.1) For merger, both Trust must have democratic structure or both must have non democratic structure
.
(16.2) If over 75% of Trustees or 75% of Voting Members of two Trusts agree for a Merger Proposal submitted by any Trustee or any Member, then and then only DR or SR or NR will execute their Merger. Merger will be done 60 days after confirming. And within 60 days, the Trustees or Members may cancel it.
.
(16.3) If two trusts have non democratic structure, then Merger Proposal must mention what will be weight of first Trust and what will be weight of the second Trust. The sum of two weights must add upto 1 or at least 0.9999. The weights will be multiplied with trustees weights in previous trusts, to get their weights in the new Merger trust.
.
(17) Fees and Taxes
.
(17.1) DR will charge 0.1% of the Trusts assets as annual fee to keep records
.
(17.2) The Income Tax and wealth tax wll apply on income and wealth of Trusts as per Income Tax and Wealth Tax act. .
.
(RTR) Right to Recall National Registrar
.
(RTR.1) If any Hindu \ Sanatan voter of India wishes to become NR, then he can appear in person before DC (DC = District Collector of District where he is voter). DC would accept his candidacy for NR. DC will take filing fee same as deposit amount for MP election. And DC will issue a serial number unique across India.
.
(RTR 2) Any Hindu \ Sanatan voter can visit Talati office in person. pay Rs 3 fee, and approve at most five persons for the NR position of NR. The Talati would enter his approvals in the computer and would give him a receipt with his voter-id#, date/time and the persons he approved. The Talati will put the preferences of the citizen on PM's website with citizen's voter-ID number and his preferences
.
(RTR.3) If a the voter comes to cancel his Approvals, the Talati will cancel one of more of his approvals without any fee
.
(RTR.4) If a candidate gets approval of over 35 crore Hindu \ Sanatan voters in India, and 1 crore more approvals then existing NR, then PM may or need not expel the existing NR and may or need not appoint the person with highest approval count as NR. The decision of PM will be final
.
(RTR.5) If existing NR has approval of less than 35 crore Hindu \ Sanatan voters, then PM may replace him.
.
(RTR.6) PM may create a system whereby citizen can file\cancel approvals using SMS or mobile phone apps or via ATMs.
.
(RTR.7) comment : Later, PM may create a system where voter can give score of (-100 to 100) to any candidate. If a voter doesn’t give any score, it will be assumed as zero. The voter will have choice --- he can give score of simply approve / not-approve, and in that case not-approving will mean score of zero and approving will mean score of 100. If a candidate has positive score from over 33% o ALL voters, then negative scores will be ignored. The candidate with highest score, and who has over 51% of highest possible score will be appointed as NR
.
(CV) Citizens’ Voice:
.
(CV.1) If any citizen residing anywhere in India wants any change in this law or has any complaint under this law, then he may submit an affidavit to DC’s designated clerk and clerk will scan the affidavit on put on PM’s website along with photo and details of the citizen. The clerk will charge Rs 30 per page. The clerk will issue serial number for the affidavit.
.
(CV.2) If any citizen residing in the area covered by the Patwari wants to register his YES / NO on the affidavit submitted in above clause, then he can go to Talati’s office and provide the serial number of the affidavit and his YES/NO on a form, and Patwari will enter his YES/NO for a fee for Rs 3 . The YES/NO shall come on PM’s website along with citizen’s voter-id, name, date etc The YES / NO count will not be binding on PM or any officer etc.
.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Fri Sep 08 2017 16:52:11 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

EIAR Act – Expelling Infiltrators and allowing in Refugees from Bangladesh, Pakistan, Sri Lanka, Myanmar etc
.
(a) to enable Govt officers to trace infiltrators and refugees for several countries, such as Bangladesh, Pakistan, Sri Lanka, Myanmar etc
.
(b) to enable Govt officers and Jurors to prove whether a person is an infiltrator or a refugee or a bonafide Indian citizen
.
(c) to expel infiltrators and allow in refugee
.
------------------------------------------
.
[ comment : This EBIAR law-draft can be printed as Gazette Notification by PM (but not by CMs). It does need approval of Loksabha or Rajyasabha and needs no amendment in Constitution.
.
This draft has 2 parts -
.
Part - I : Basic instructions for citizens
.
Part - II : Other instructions for Officers, citizens and comments
.
Important clauses are – (1.2) , (1.3) , (5.4) , (6.6) , (6.8) , (6.8.1) to (6.8.5) , (8.1) , (8.2)
.
The comments listed are not binding on any Officer / Minister / judges. The Jurors , Citizens and all can use it as guidelines. But they are not binding. ]
.
-----------------------------------
Part - I : Basic instructions for citizens
------------------------------------
.
(1) comment : summary of the law
.
(1.1) comment : This law sets a procedure by which Central govt officers can trace Bangladeshi infiltrators as well as refugees and gather evidences to prove / disapprove whether he is Bangladeshi or a bonafide Indian citizen, and whether he is infiltrator or a refugee.
.
(1.2) comment : The officers will gather phone call logs, blood group details and DNA details of accused and their blood relatives in Bangladesh. The officers will also gather whatever documents they can gather from Bangladesh that can prove that accused is Bangladeshi or not.
.
(1.3) comment : The officers will place all facts before Jurors. The Jurors using these facts, testimonies and using narcotest in public, the Jurors will decide whether the accused is Bangladeshi or bonafide Indian citizen, and whether he is refugee or infiltrator. And if he is infiltrator, then Jury will decide the punishment.
.
(2) NR and Right to Recall NR
.
(2.1) PM will appoint National Registrar for Infiltrators and Refugees, also referred as NRIR, or NR for short. NR will appoint State Registrars and District Registrars, and other officers to carry out the tasks
.
.(2.2) If you (you means, every registered voter of India) are dissatisfied with will be able to approve upto 5 alternate candidates. If any candidate gets approved by over 45 crore voters of India, then PM may appoint the most approved candidate as NR. Thus, you i.e. voters of India, will have Right to Recall NR. The details of the procedure are in “(RTR) Right to recall NR”: clauses.
.
-----------------------------------
Part - II : Instructions for Citizens, Officers and comments
------------------------------------
.
(3) PM will appoint an officer titled as National Registrar for Infiltrators and Refugees, henceforth referred as NRIR or simply NR. The voters may replace NR using “{RTR) Right to Recall NR”
.
(4) National Registrar will appoint State Registrars i.e. SRs and District Registrars i.e. DRs, and NR will also print RTR procedure code for SR and DR NR / SR / DR will hire other officers on at most 2 year contract or take officers from State / Central Govt on deputation
.
(5) Making first list of Bangladeshi using phone call logs and other details
.
(5.1) PM will order Department of Telecom to provide list of all calls to and from Bangladesh to NR in past 2 years. Using this list, NR will make a list of Bangladeshi suspects
.
(5.2) Any citizen of India can publicly or privately provide a list of Bangladeshi suspects, and he must order the list in descending order of suspicion. If the list has less than 20% errors, then NR will reward him in private pr public, as the citizen wishes
.
(5.3) NR, based on his discretion and to the best of his information, will give approximate number of Bangladeshi in each District in India
.
(5.4) NR will appoints agents in Bangladesh. NR will agents to get blood group details and DNA samples of suspects’ relatives living in Bangladesh. NR will also ask agents to get documents that may prove Bangladeshi origin of suspects.
.
(5.5) NR may put names, pictures and other details of the suspects on the public website, requesting all to obtain names etc of his relatives in Bangladesh.
.
(5.6) NR will prepare a list of suspects that NR is convinced are Bangladeshi.
.
(6) Jury Trials to decide if suspects are infiltrators or refugee
.
(6.1) For each suspect, NR will select 100 voters between age of 30 years and 55 years from 5 different districts. The first district will be district closest to where suspect was found, and has Bangladeshi population below 10% (best to the information of NR). The other 4 districts will be four closest districts to the first district and each district should also have Bangladeshi population below 10%.
.
(6.2) NR will select 20 voters at random from each District.
.
(6.3) The NR may increase number of districts to 10, and select 20 more Jurors from each Districts using above method. NR’s decision on number of Districts to be chosen will be final.
.
(6.4) The accused will inform Jurors about whether he was born in India or in which country was he born in. And if he was borne in India, the in which city / village he was borne, whereabouts of his parents, brothers, relatives and where he did schooling etc. And if he was borne outside India, then why he has emigrated to India.
.
(6.5) NR’s officers will explain whether the person is borne in India or borne outside India, and whether he is an infiltrator or a refugee.
.
(6.6) If over 50 Jurors suggest that polygraph in public, brainmapping in public and narcotest in public should be taken, then these tests will be taken. Each Juror will suggest questions and each Jurors will give 0-100 Points to each question and questions with highest marks will be asked. NR will select doctor for narotest and brainmapping. But of over 67 Jurors approve another doctor, then that doctor will be taken. The doctor chosen must have degrees required by NR.
.
(6.7) After hearing all sides, each Juror will cite whether he thinks that accused is bonafide Indian citizen or refugee or infiltrator. And if infiltrator, then for how many months should he be imprisoned for wrongful entry inside India, and what should be fine on him. And if the accused was refugee and bonafide citizen, then what should be his compensation between Rs 0 and Rs 100,000
.
(6.8) Decision whether he is citizen, refugee or infiltrator
.
(6.8.1) If over 67% Jurors declare than accused is infiltrator, then NR will imprison him for median number of months, and then deport him to the country where he came from
.
(6.8.2) if over 50% but below 67% Jurors cite that he is infiltrator, then he will be allowed to stay in India with no imprisonment, but in one of the districts decided by NR
.
(6.8.3) if 50% or less Jurors decide that he is infiltrator, then he will not be punished.
.
(6.8.4) if over 50% Jurors declare him as citizen, then there will be no action against and no restrictions against him. And NR will give median amount as compensation
.
(6.8.5) if over 50% have declared him as non-citizen, but a refugee, then he can stay in India as resident, in a district designated by NR ; and later, he may apply for citizenship
.
(7) Appeals
.
(7.1) Either NR or convict may appeal the decision of Jurors before Jury chosen from twice the number of districts. From each district, NR will choose 20 Jurors at random.
.
(7.2) The decision of second Jury can be appealed in High Court or Supreme Court.

(8) Reduction in prison sentence
.
(8.1) NR’s officer will ask convict to provide names and addresses of blood relatives For every name and address of blood relatives or other infiltrators he gives, and proofs he provides, NR will reduce sentence by 1 month.
.
(8.2) NR’s officers can narcotest convict in public to obtain more names of infiltrators that he knows of.
.
(9) Districts for refugees – to divide burden of refugees across India
.
(9.1) NR will make a list of districts where population of refugees is less. (comment : so all districts of Asam, West Bengal, North East, Mumbai, Delhi, Gurgaon, Noida etc will not come in this list, as they have already taken more than fair share of refugees)
.
(9.2) NR will remove border districts and districts where economic opportunities are low. (comment : border districts have security problems, and districts where economic opportunities are low will not be able to support refugees)
.
(9.3) The refugees will have to live in one of the districts in the list.
.
(RTR) Right to Recall National Registrar for Infiltrators and Refugees
.
(RTR.1) If any voter of India wishes to become NR, then he can appear in person before DC (DC = District Collector of District where he is voter). DC would accept his candidacy for NR. DC will take filing fee same as deposit amount for MP election. And DC will issue a serial number unique across India.
.
(RTR 2) Any voter can visit Talati office in person. pay Rs 3 fee, and approve at most five persons for the NR position of NR. The Talati would enter his approvals in the computer and would give him a receipt with his voter-id#, date/time and the persons he approved. The Talati will put the preferences of the citizen on PM's website with citizen's voter-ID number and his preferences
.
(RTR.3) If a the voter comes to cancel his Approvals, the Talati will cancel one of more of his approvals without any fee
.
(RTR.4) If a candidate gets approval of over 43 crore voters in India, and has 1 crore more approvals than existing NR, then PM may or need not expel the existing NR and may or need not appoint the person with highest approval count as NR. The decision of PM will be final
.
(RTR.5) If existing NR has approval of less than 35 crore voters, then PM may replace him.
.
(RTR.6) PM may create a system whereby citizen can file\cancel approvals using SMS or mobile phone apps or via ATMs.
.
(RTR.7) comment : Later, PM may create a system where voter can give score of (-100 to 100) to any candidate. If a voter doesn’t give any score, it will be assumed as zero. The voter will have choice --- he can give score of simply approve / not-approve, and in that case not-approving will mean score of zero and approving will mean score of 100. If a candidate has positive score from over 33% o ALL voters, then negative scores will be ignored. The candidate with highest score, and who has over 51% of highest possible score will be appointed as NR
.
(CV) Citizens’ Voice:
.
(CV.1) If any citizen residing anywhere in India wants any change in this law or has any complaint under this law, then he may submit an affidavit to DC’s designated clerk and clerk will scan the affidavit on put on PM’s website along with photo and details of the citizen. The clerk will charge Rs 30 per page. The clerk will issue serial number for the affidavit.
.
(CV.2) If any citizen residing in the area covered by the Patwari wants to register his YES / NO on the affidavit submitted in above clause, then he can go to Talati’s office and provide the serial number of the affidavit and his YES/NO on a form, and Patwari will enter his YES/NO for a fee for Rs 3 . The YES/NO shall come on PM’s website along with citizen’s voter-id, name, date etc The YES / NO count will not be binding on PM or any officer etc.
.

(1) who is in power --- before and behind the curtain
.

.

.

.

.

.

.

.

.

राम रहीम की 14 कंपनियाें का 800 करोड़ का कारोबार बंद, 8 हजार लोग बेराेजगार
.
7-sep-2017

गुरमीत के जेल जाते ही सभी कंपनियाें, 8 स्कूल-कॉलेजों, होटल-रेस्टोरेंट और 52 दुकानों पर ताले, बैंक खाते सील।
.
राम रहीम की 14 कंपनियाें का 800 करोड़ का कारोबार बंद, 8 हजार लोग बेराेजगार
.
जालंधर. 28 अगस्त को रेप केस में दोषी गुरमीत राम रहीम को 20 साल की सजा होने के बाद 10 दिन के अंदर ही डेरे का करीब 800 करोड़ का कारोबार बंद हो गया है। सिरसा डेरे की 14 कंपनियां, 8 स्कूल-कॉलेज, फाइव स्टार होटल एमएसजी रिजॉर्ट, कशिश रेस्टोरेंट, पुराने डेरे के सामने एसी सुपर मार्केट की 52 दुकानों पर ताले लगे हैं। कई बैंक खाते भी सील कर दिए गए हैं। कारोबार से जुड़े करीब आठ हजार लोग बेरोजगार हो गए हैं और डर के मारे सिरसा छोड़ गए हैं। साथ ही देशभर में 400 के करीब डीलर्स ने एमएसजी स्टोर्स बंद कर दिए हैं। 9 कंपनियां पिछले 4 साल में खड़ी की हैं...
.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sun Sep 10 2017 04:22:16 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Who are "usuk-elitemen" that I often mention? And how do they control Indian leaders / judges?
.
(1) Who are "usuk-elitemen" that I often mention?
.
Eliteman means a person who is extremely wealthy or earning lot, and yet manages to pay much lesser TOTAL taxes , as % of his wealth / income, than tax rate that middle class men pay.
.
So a doctor earning Rs 10 crore a year, and paying Rs 2 crore taxes is NOT an eliteman, But someone who has land worth Rs 500,000 crores in Mumbai alone, and not paying even Rs 10000 crore as TOTAL income and all taxes is an eliteman.
.
The elitemen has stashed their wealth in mineral mines, foundations aka charitable trusts, foreign companies, foreign accounts, bank holding companies, banks shares etc and so defacto tax rate on their income/wealth is much much below middle class and even lower class.
.
Each entity they own, the tax rate is less !! eg if you own an emmpty unused home in USA, then normal tax rate applies. But if a bank-owner owns home via bank he owns, the tax rate is almost zero !! In addition bank owners via bank can create unlimited money and loan it to companies they own, and so defacto interest they have to pay is low. And the foundations owners stash the wealth in foundations, and so inheritance tax rate is zero, income tax rate is near zero, many wealth tax exemptions apply and so on !!!
.
So who are these usuk-elitemen at INDIVIDUAL levels? What are their names, addresses, social security numbers etc?
.
To get complete answer, one will will need to publish ALL data on who owns which mineral mines, who owns which plots of land, who owns which foundations, and how much wealth those foundations have, who owns which bank holding companies and so on. Once this data is all put on public website, all names can be churned out
.
Who are usuk-elitemen? Some families are Rothschilde, Rockefellar etc. Some estimate that some 200 families and some 2000 individuals own over 90% of bank holding companies and foundation assets.
.
India too has its share of wealth individuals whom total tax rate is near much much lower and defacto mineral royalty they pay is much lower. eg Tata owns a coal mine on which royalty he pays is (hold your breadth) Re 1 per YEAR per ACRE !! Yes, per acre, not even ton. Dig as much coal as you want and royalty is princely sum of Re 1 per year per acre only !! He got this mine in 1946 for 999 year lease !!! Condition is that coal can be used only for Tata's steel mills. But who is watching the gates? Tata's own security !!! And likewise, Godrej via trusts owns some 3400 acres of land in mumbai alone !! At throw away price of Rs 500,000 per sqmt, it would be about Rs 500,000 lakh crores !! And how much wealth tax he payd? zero !! because it is "charitable" trust.
.
What makes them stronger inside their own country? The strength comes from LOWER TAX RATE they pay and lower royalty rates and land rents (or land prices) on govt plots they pay. Then other ways are bribing forcing ministers / ias / judges to twist laws and judgments that will make the field unlevel and so on.
.
---
.
(2) Why usuk-elitemen are stronger than Indian-elitemen?
.
Thats because USA / UK are far far stronger than India. USA has RTR, JurySys, referendum, multi-election, no service tax, no GST, less sales tax, no excise, has less convoluted income tax, has wealth tax etc and so on. India has no RTR, no JurySys, no referendum, no multi-eelections, has huge GST etc and no wealth tax. And so factory owners in USA are LESS INEFFICIENT than factory owners in India. And so USA's manufacturing ability is more and weapon manufacturing ability is more.
.
So Indian elitemen are now dead scared of usuk-elitemen. If Indian elitemen defy the order of usuk-elitemen, then they know that usuk-elitemen wll give weapons to pakistan and run havoc in India (eg kargil war).
.
In short, the strength of usuk-elitemen comes from weapons US Govt owns
.
Now inside USA, there is RTR / Jury / referendum etc to by which commons control us-elitemen, But when it comes to us-elitemen's power in FOREIGN country such as Iraq or Libya or India, the ONLY control is weapons that these countries own, which are next to zero. So us-elitemen have effectively NO control in Iraq, Libya, India etc
.
-----
.
(3) Do elitemen add wealth of subtract wealth from commons?
.
The elitemen pay less tax rate and less royalties and less land price (from govt land). So they subtract wealth from commons. BUT, there is a big BUT here --- usuk-elitemen also get gigatons of wealth from foreign countries into their own countries. eg us-govt and us-elitemen got gigatons of crude oil from iraq and libya for free !! So this adds wealth to commons. Thats why activists in USA are not juch against us-elitemen,. Because they do all additons and all subtractions, they see that commons of usa are not losing
.
The commons in country like iraq, libya , china etc end up facing twice the burden --- they have support their own elitemen and also usuk-elitemen. Solution? Long list
.
-----
.
(4) So summing up, usuk-elitemen are NOT wealthy men in USA who are paying huge taxes too. They are people who have huge incomes / wealth via their companies / trusts and defacto tax rate they pay in usa or foreign country where they are earning is much much lower than tax rate on middle class respective country. Their strength in their country mainly comes from lower tax rate and in foreign country, it comes from weapons they and their own govts own.
.
solution for us-activists? No suggestions
.
solution of Indian activists? They should work to bring laws such as RTR, JurySys, remove GST, remove excise , service tax etc, bring wealth tax on all wealths including wealths owned by charities. And they should inform all Indian voters that SoGa / NaMo / Arke and congress / rss / aap workers oppose these laws. ...

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sun Sep 10 2017 12:01:35 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Proposed law for Jury System

------------------
DJSM Act – District Jury System for Murder and other violent crimes
.
Once this act is passed, punishments / fines / acquittals, in cases such as murder, abduction, rape, beatings, death due to negligence etc will be decided by 15 voters to 1500 voters chosen at RANDOM from District / State or India, between the age of 25 years and 55 years. And later, PM / CM may add types of crimes and types of civil disputes that will be decided by Jurors and not judges
---------------------
.
[ comment : This DJSM law-draft needs to be passed in Loksabha and Rajyasabha with simple majority, and passing this law needs no amendment in Constitution.
.
This draft has 2 parts -
.
Part - I : Basic instructions for citizens
.
Part - II : Other instructions for Officers, citizens and comments
.
Important clauses are – (1.2) , (2) , (2.1) to (2.5) , (4.1) , (4.2) , (8.1) , (9) , (12) , (13) , (14) , (17) , (19) , (20) , (28) , (28.1)
.
The “comments” listed here are not binding on any Officer / Minister / judges. The Jurors , Citizens and all can use it as guidelines. But they are not binding. ]
.
-----------------------------------
Part - I : Basic instructions for citizens
------------------------------------
.
(1) comment : summary of the law
.
(1.1) comment : This law creates Jury System in Magistrates’ Courts District Courts and Sessions Courts, for crimes such as murder, beatings, threat, rape, abduction and all crimes where physical force or threat of using physical violence is used.
.
(1.2) comment : once this law passes, you (you means registered voter in India) can be called for Jury Duty. In Jury Duty, you will have to listen to facts and arguments as presented by accused, victim, witnesses and lawyers of both sides and decide the punishment/fines or acquittal.
.
(1.3) comment : The jury system will be supervised by an officer called as District Jury Administrator or DJA. DJA will be appointed by CM ad you (you means voters of District) can replace him using “(RTR) Right to Recall DJA” clauses given in this law.
.
(1.3.1) comment : In “Right to recall District Jury Administrator”, you can approve at most 5 candidates anyday and he may cancel approval of any candidate anyday. And you need not file any approval if you don’t wish to.
.
(1.3.2) comment : The candidate which has highest approval and approval of over 35% of all voters in district, will replace existing DJA and become new DJA.
.
-----------------------------------
Part - II : Instructions for Citizens, Officers and comments
------------------------------------
.
(2) This law will cover only following cases, regardless of age of accused and victims
.
(2.1)murder, attempt to murder, any human death due to accident or negligence or any unnatural human death
(2.2) any crime involving violence, such as beating, or threat of violence or accident or negligence that caused bodily harm or could have caused serious injury
(2.3) abduction / rape / molestation / harassment / stalking of woman of any age, including harassment at workplace.
(2.4) any type of crime or any type of civil dispute mentioned by PM or CM using Gazette Notification in this list
(2.5) any crime or any type of civil dispute mentioned by majority voters via using “(CV) Citizen’s Voice” clauses, and approved by PM / CM.
.
(3) If a type of crime or a type of civil dispute is added by CM, then it will apply only for that state. If it is added by PM, then it will apply for whole nation or States specified by PM in that GN. The CM / PM may specify whether Jurors will be chosen from District / State or neighboring Districts/ States or whole of India.
.
(3.1) comment : This law, at start, will only cover some crimes where the argument that - “Jurors don’t understand law about such and such crime” – has been rejected as outright lie by majority voters. Later, PM / CM or voters can add types of crimes as well as types of civil disputes where they believe that argument that “Jurors don’t understand law involving such and such crime” has been rejected as outright lie by most voters of India. eg PM / CM / voters may add crimes like gohatya, corruption, nepotism, theft. fraud, cheque bounce, loan defaults, forgery of land sale documents and so on
.
(3.2) comment : PM / CM may also add types of civil offences .such as tenant / owner dispute, labor disputes and so on, where PM / CM / voters see that laws and matter are simple enough that Jurors do understand, and the argument that argument that - “Jurors don’t understand law involving such and such crime” has been rejected as lie by most voters of India.
.
(4) Temporary suspension of Jury System in a District
.
(4.1) With approval of over 51% of ALL citizen voters in that State, CM can suspend all above clauses and appoint his own DJA for 5 years in one or more districts. CM may change DJA anytime during these 5 years. And CM may move a case of that district to any other district of selected at random
.
(4.2) With approval of over 51% of ALL citizen voters in India, PM can suspend all above clauses for any districts in India and appoint DJA for 5 years in those districts. PM may change DJA anytime during these 5 years. And PM may move a case of that district of a neighboring state’s district selected at random
.
(5) The CM will appoint an officer as DJA for each District., which citizens can replace using Right to recall DJA clauses given later
.
(6) Formation of First Grand Jury - Using the voter list, DJA will, in a public meeting, randomly select 50 citizens from the voter-list between age of 25 years and 55 years. From that, he can exclude any 20 after interview, so that finally there are 30 Grand Jurors. If DJA is appointed by CM or PM under clause-(4), then DJA may select up to 100 citizens from District / State / India and exclude 70.
.
(7) Formation of subsequent Grand Juries - In the first set of Grand Jurors, DJA will retire the first 10 Grand Jurors every 10 days. And after first month, term of each Grand Juror will be 3 months, and thus 10 Grand Jurors will retire every month and 10 more will be selected. For selection of 10 Grand Jurors, DJA will select 20 voters at random from voter list of District or State or India, as in clause above, and select any 10 after interview.
.
(8) Method to select voters at random -
.
(8.1) The DJA will not use any electronic device to select a number randomly. He will use the procedure detailed by CM or PM. If CM or PM have not specified the procedure, he will select as follow.
.
(8.2) Say DJA has to choose a number between 1 and a four digit number say ABCD. Then DJA will do 4 rounds of dice-throw for each digit. In a round if the digit he needs to select is between 0-5, then DJA will use only 1 dice and if the digit he needs to select is between 0-9, he will use 2 dices.
.
(8.3) The number selected will be 1 less than the number which comes in case of single-dice throw and 2 less in case of double-dice throw. If throw of dices exceeds the highest digit he needs, he will throw the dices again.
.
(8.3.1) Example - Suppose DJA needs to select a page in a book, which has 3693 pages. Then DJA will execute 4 rounds. In the 1st round he will use 1 dice as he needs to select a number between 0-3. If the dice shows 5 or 6, he will throw the dice again. If the dice show 3, the number selected is 3-1=2. And DJA will proceed to second round. In the second round, he needs to select a number between 0-6. So he will throw two dices. If the sum exceeds 8, he will throw the dices again. If the sum is suppose 6, the second digit selected is 6 - 2 = 4. Like that, suppose the dices in 4 rounds show 3, 5, 10 and 2. Then DJA will select digits as (3 - 1), (5 - 2), (10 - 2), (2 - 1) i.e. page number 2381. The DJA should use different citizens to throw dices. Suppose the voter-list has B books, the largest book has P pages and all pages have N entries. Then using above method or method described by CM, DJA will select 3 random numbers between (1 to B), (1 to P) and (1 to N). Now suppose selected book has less than that many pages or the selected page has fewer entries. Then he will again select a numbers between (1 to B), (1 to P) and (1 to N).
.
(8.3.2) Another method can be - DJA can prepare list of all voters, and select two large prime numbers at random. Say there are N voters. Then DJA can select two prime numbers between N/2 and 2N say n and m. And the voters selected can be (n mod N), (n + m) mod N, (n + 2m) mod N to (n + (k-1)*m) mod N, where k is number of persons to be selected.
.
(8.3.3) If Jury System is suspended in District, and DJA is appointed by CM or PM , then he may select voters from whole State or India. The age will be between 25 years and 55 years.
.
(8.4) The Grand Jurors will meet on every Saturday and Sunday. They may meet on more days if over 15 Grand Jurors approve. The number must be over 15, even when less than 30 Grand Jurors are present. The meetings, if happen, must start at 11 am and last till at least 5 pm. The Grand Juror will get Rs. 500 per day he attends plus travel expense. The CM , PM may change the compensation as per inflation or other conditions such as travel distance. The money will be give 60 days after his term ends.
.
(8.5) If a Grand Juror is absent in a meeting, he will not his daily payment. And may loose up to thrice his amount to be paid and maximum fine of 0.05% of his wealth and 1% of his annual income may be imposed. The individuals who are Grand Jurors 60 days later will decide the fine.
.
(9) If any person or company or District Prosecutor or any entity, has complaint against any other person or entity, and crime is covered under clause-2 or GN issued under clause-2, then he can write to some or all Grand Jurors or ask Grand Jurors to give hearing or he may upload the complain on website using (CV) clause. The complainer may specify the remedy he wishes. The remedy can be obtaining possession of a property or obtaining monetary compensation from the accused or imprisoning the accused for certain number of years/months or his execution. The remedy asked must be legal, as per the law interpreted by Jurors and Grand Jurors.
.
(10) If over 15 Grand Jurors, in a meeting, issue an invitation to a witness or complainer or accused, then that person may appear. The Grand Jurors may or need not invite the accused or complainer. They may allow lawyers and experts to speak or not to speak.
.
(11) If over 15 Grand Jurors declare that there is some merit in the complaint, then the DJA will call a Jury consisting of 15 to 1500 citizens, between the age of 25 years and 55 years, to examine the complaint. And if 15 or more Grand Jurors say that “case has no merit”, then the complain will be dismissed
.
(12) if majority Grand Jurors think that the complaint is absolutely frivolous then they may impose a fine of upto Rs 5000 per hour they spent in hearing and deliberation, within one hour minimum, and no more. Each Grand Juror will declare a fine, and the median fine will be imposed and executed by DJA. The Jurors will also decide how much of that fine should be given to wrongfully accused as compensation. The accused may file a separate case to recover additional compensation for damages such as loss of time, reputation and other damages he suffered.
.
(13) Deciding number of Jurors for a case - Each Grand Juror will propose the number of Jurors needed for the case, and DJA will take the median as the decision. In case number of Grand Jurors are even, then DJA will take the higher median. Each Juror will propose a number between 15 and 1500. The guideline for Grand Jurors are –
.
(13.1) if the accused has higher economic or social or political standing, then the number of Jurors may be higher.
.
(13.2) if the crime is serious, the number of Jurors should be higher. eg if crime is theft of say Rs 100000 or less, then number of Jurors may be 15. If amount is more, then it will be higher. And if the alleged crime is murder, then number of Jurors may be 50 or 100 or more.
.
(13.3) if the person is accused of multiple crimes in past, and Grand Jurors see merit in most complaints, then they may put higher number of Jurors. eg if a person appears as career criminal and has been accused for several beatings or murders in past, then number of Jurors against him can be 100 or even 1500.
.
(13.4) if amount involved is large, then number of Jurors may be more. Number should be 15 at least. And 1 more Juror for every Rs 1 crore may be added. The maximum can be 1500 Jurors.
.
(13.5) The decision of Grand Jurors on number of Jurors will be final.
.
(13.6) The Grand Jurors will decide whether the Jurors will be from District, State or India.
.
(14) Jury selection - The DJA will randomly select number of citizens equal to twice the number of Jurors required from the voter lists. Then DJA will then send them summons to appear. And of those who arrive, DJA will excuse the Jurors who are relatives or neighbors or colleagues of either party or their lawyers. And he will also excuse Jurors who have served in the District in past 10 years. Then DJA will select required number of Jurors at random, not using any interview. The decision on excusing will be taken by DJA and can be changed only by majority of Grand Jurors.
.
(14.1) DJA will inform each Jurors about academic scores of each Jurors, and their profession and wealth / income levels. The Jurors who are less informed and less skilled in logic / maths, may take advice from Jurors who are more skilled in logic / maths. The discretion of each Juror on which Juror’s advice he will take will be final.
.
(15) DJA will ask CDJ = Chief District Judge to appoint one or at most 3 Judges to advise the Jurors and administer the case. The Chief Judge's decision on number of judges will be final. DJA will appoint Trial Administrator , TA henceforth, to run the trial.
.
(16) The trial will go from 11am to 5pm. The trial will start only after all Jurors are present or over 75% of the Jurors selected (not just present) vote for hearing to start. In all clauses, the majority means majority of Jurors selected, not just present, and “x% of Jurors” means “x% of all Jurors”, not just Jurors present.
.
(17) TA, i.e. Trial administrator appointed by DJA, will allow the complainer to speak for 1 hour, during which no can interrupt. Then Judge will allow the accused to speak for 1 hour and 30 minutes during which no one can interrupt. Like this, the Judge will alternate the speakers. The lunch break will start between 1 pm and 2pm and will be for 1 hour. The case will go on like this on every day. The Jurors with majority vote can change the duration for which one may speak.
.
(18) The case will go for at least 2 days. On the 3rd day or later, if majority Jurors declare that they have heard enough, then the hearings will go on for 1 more day. If on the next day, majority Jurors declare that they would like to hear more arguments, then the case will go on till majority Jurors say that case should end.
.
(19) On the last day, after both parties have presented the case for 1 hour each, the Jurors will deliberate for at least 2 hours. If after 2 hours, majority Jurors say that they need no more deliberation, then TA will ask each Juror to publicly declare his verdict.
.
(20) In case of fine, each Juror will state the fine he thinks is appropriate, and must be less than the legal limit. If it is higher than legal limit, then TA must take it as legal limit. TA will arrange the fine amounts stated in decreasing order, i.e. highest first and lowest last. And TA will take the fine approved by over 2/3rd majority. eg if there are 100 Jurors, then 67th fine amount in decreasing order will be taken as the fine collectively imposed by the Jury. So if 34 out of 100 Jurors have declared zero fine, then fine will be zero.
.
(21) In case of prison sentence, TA will arrange the imprisonment lengths cited by Jurors in decreasing order i.e. highest first and lowest last. If sentence length is higher than legal limit then maximum sentence as stated in the Law will be taken. And TA will take prison sentence approved by over 2/3rd of Jurors as prison sentence collectively decided by the Jury. So if 1/3rd Jurors have declared zero months of prison, then and then only accused will be declared not guilty and set free. eg if there are 100 Jurors, then 67th imprisonment length in decreasing order will be taken as the imprisonment. So if 34 Jurors say zero months, then punishment will be zero months.
.
(22) In case punishment of execution, over 75% of all Jurors must approve execution. In such case, there will automatic second trial. The second Jury will only decide if the convict should be executed or not. In second Jury too, at least 75% of all Jurors must approve execution.
.
(23) In case of any other remedy asked, the Jurors will ask both parties to submit at most 5 proposals or less. The Jurors may take proposal from judges or TA or any person approved by Jurors. Each Juror will give marks from 0 to 100 to each proposal. Not giving any marks would mean 0. The proposal with highest total marks would be taken as proposal approved by the Jurors.
.
(23) DJA will give same compensation to Jurors as Grand Jurors
.
(24) Fining a Juror for being absent or late - in case a Juror or a party does not show up or shows up late, the Grand Jurors after 3 months will decide the fine, which can be upto 0.1% of his wealth, or 1% of his annual income. The Grand Jurors decision will be final.
.
(25) If over 50% of Jurors think that complain was frivolous, then each Juror may impose a fine of Rs 1000 per day per Juror and no more. Each Juror will disclose a fine, and median fine will be collected by DJA. But limit on fine will be 2% of the wealth of complainer or 10% of his annual income, whichever is higher. Of this, accused will get amount equal to number of days he spent multiplied by his daily income in past income tax return. If the accused wants more compensation, then he may file a separate complain.
.
(26) In complex cases, each party or TA may ask any technical / legal expert , who is willing to give information, to explains facts and law to the Jurors. DJA will decide his daily compensation, which will not exceed his daily income.
.
(27) DJA can create a Grand Jury at tahsil level and also run Jury Trial in tahsil courts, if that District has tahsil Courts. The Tahsil Court Grand Jury and Tahsil Court Jury will hear the cases that fall within them. DJA will appoint a TJA = Tahsil Jury administrator for Tahsil Courts. And operations will run in same way as described in above clauses.
.
(28) Appeal : Either party will have 30 days to appeal against the verdict in the State's High Court or the Supreme Court of India. The verdict of Higher Court will prevail over verdict of District Court.
.
(28.1) comment : This law makes no imperative on whether High Court or Supreme Court will use judge system or Jury System. If MPs or voters want Jury System in High Court or Supreme Court, then they may print a separate law.
.
(29) If citizens of district or state or India order District Principal Judge to cancel the verdict of District Court Jury using CV clauses at District / State or India level, DPCJ may cancel the verdict of Jury.
.
(30) The District Police Chief or policemen designated by him will execute the fine or imprisonments as written by the Judge and approved by the Jurors.
.
(31) In cases listed in clause-2 or GN issued under clause-2, no Govt employee will impose any fine or prison sentence without consent of over 2/3rd of Jurors of the Lower Courts, unless approved High Courts or Supreme Court or majority voters of District / State / India as decided by (CV) clauses. No Govt employee will imprison any citizen for more than 48 hours without approval of over 2/3rd of the Jurors or 50% of the Grand Jurors or majority voters of District / State / India using (CV) clauses
.
(32) The Jurors will decide the facts as well as intensions, and must also interpret the laws as well the Constitution, and natural justice. If Jurors think that the law is unjust or inapplicable, then the Jurors can give “not guilty” verdict even against the law.
.
(RTR) Right to Recall District Jury administrator
.
(RTR.1) If any voter of India above age of 30 years wishes to become DJA, then he can appear in person before any DC (DC = District Collector where he wishes to become DJA). DC would accept his candidacy for DJA. DJA will take filing fee same as deposit amount for MP election And DC will issue a serial number unique across India.
.
(RTR 2) Any voter can visit Talati office in person. pay Rs 3 fee, and approve at most five persons for DJA position. The Talati would enter his approvals in the computer and would give him a receipt with his voter-id#, date/time and the persons he approved. The Talati will put the preferences of the citizen on PM's website with citizen's voter-ID number and his preferences
.
(RTR.3) If a the voter comes to cancel his Approvals, the Talati will cancel one of more of his approvals without any fee
.
(RTR.4) If a candidate gets approval of over 35% of ALL voters in District, and 2% more than existing DJA, then CM may or need not expel the existing DJA and may or need not appoint the person with highest approval count as DJA. The decision of CM will be final
.
(RTR.5) If existing DJA has approval of less than 35% of ALL voters, then CM may replace him.
.
(RTR.6) PM may create a system whereby citizen can file\cancel approvals using SMS or mobile phone apps or via ATMs.
.
(RTR.7) comment : Later, PM may create a system where voter can give score of (-100 to 100) to any candidate. If a voter doesn’t give any score, it will be assumed as zero. The voter will have choice --- he can give score of simply approve / not-approve, and in that case not-approving will mean score of zero and approving will mean score of 100. If a candidate has positive score from over 33% o ALL voters, then negative scores will be ignored. The candidate with highest score, and who has over 51% of highest possible score may be appointed as DJA
.
(CV) Citizens’ Voice:
.
(CV.1) If any citizen residing anywhere in India wants any change in this law or has any complaint under this law, then he may submit an affidavit to DC’s designated clerk and clerk will scan the affidavit on put on PM’s website along with photo and details of the citizen. The clerk will charge Rs 30 per page. The clerk will issue serial number for the affidavit.
.
(CV.2) If any citizen wants to register his YES / NO on the affidavit submitted in above clause, then he can go to Talati’s office and provide the serial number of the affidavit and his YES/NO on a form, and Patwari will enter his YES/NO for a fee for Rs 3 . The YES/NO shall come on PM’s website along with citizen’s voter-id, name, date etc The YES / NO count will not be binding on PM or any officer etc. PM may enable citizen file YES / NO using SMS or APP.

--------
.
As per proposed law, crimes such as murder, attempt to murder, death due to negligence, rape, abduction, harassment , stalking etc will be tried by Jurors and not judges,. For such cases, 15 voters to 1500 voters between age of 25 years and 55 years will be chosen from voter list of District / State or whole India. These voters aka Jurors will listen to both sides, their lawyers, technical experts and decide the punishment. The punishment taken will be "median" of punishments decided by Jurors/
.
This proposed law creates Jury System ONLY in lower courts,. A separate proposed law will create Jury System in State High Courts and also Supreme Court

Pawan Jury BhilwaraRj

Sun Sep 10 2017 16:38:24 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

डेरा समर्थक कभी भी किसी प्रकार की हिंसा एवं देश विरोधी गतिविधियों में शामिल नहीं रहे। बावजूद इसके, सोनिया / मोदी / केजरीवाल / भागवत / जज एवं इनके अंध भगत डेरे के व्यवस्थागत ढाँचे को कतरा कतरा तोड़ने के सभी प्रयास कर रहे है। माहौल कुछ इस तरह का बना दिया गया है जैसे डेरा और उनके समर्थक देश विरोधी एवं अलगाव वादी है !!!
.
ओह माय गॉड फिल्म कान्हा vs कान्हा नामक नाटक पर लिखी गयी है। परेश रावल ने इसमें मुख्य भूमिका भी निभायी है और वे इसके सह निर्माता भी है। इस नाटक के फायनेंसर संघ के नेता है और यह नाटक के कई शो देश भर में संघ के कार्यकर्ताओ के लिए आयोजित किये गए है। स्वाभाविक रूप से संघ के कार्यकर्ता इस नाटक और इस पर बनी फिल्म के प्रशंसक है !!
.
संघ के कार्यकताओ के बीच एक और स्वामी काफी लोकप्रिय है -- स्वामी सच्चिदानंद। उनके लेखन का केंद्रीय सन्देश यही रहता है कि , "विभिन्न सम्प्रदाय हिन्दुओ का विभाजन करके उन्हें कमजोर कर रहे है" !!! अत: मजबूत होने के लिए हिन्दुओ को "एक" हो जाना चाहिए !! इसके अलावा वे यह भी स्थापित करते है कि सभी गुरु वगैरह हिन्दू धर्म को कमजोर कर रहे है !!!
.
यदि आप पंक्तियों एवं शब्दों के बीच छुपे हुए निहितार्थ को पढ़ सकते है तो साफ़ तौर पर समझ सकते है कि वे कहना क्या चाहते है।
.
ओह माय गॉड फिल्म भी इसी तरह का सन्देश देती है -- सभी हिन्दू गुरु फ्रॉड और ढकोसले बाज है !! और इसीलिए सभी हिन्दू गुरुओ को ठिकाने लगा देना चाहिए !!!
.
लेकिन क्यों ? आखिर ये सभी गुरु / संत फ्रॉड और बुरे क्यों है ? क्या ये लोग कश्मीरी आतंकियों की तरह आतंकी गतिविधियां संचालित कर रहे है ? क्या ले लोग नक्सलियों की तरह पुलिस वालो को मार रहे है ? क्या ये लोग दाऊद की तरह लोगो को लूट रहे है और उनकी हत्याएं कर रहे है ? क्या ये लोग अपने आस पास रहने वाले लोगो को कश्मीरी पंडितो की तरह मारकर भगा देते है ?
.
नहीं। लेकिन फिर भी सोनिया / मोदी / केजरीवाल जैसे ओह माय गॉड फ़िल्म के प्रशंषको की नजर में ये सभी संत बुरे और फ्रॉड है !!!
.
संत आसाराम जी बापू, श्री गुरमीत राम रहीम जी, संत रामपाल जी जैसे संतो ने कभी भी हिंसा को प्रेरित नहीं किया। इन संतो पर कर चोरी या जमीने हड़पने के मामले लगभग नगण्य है। इन्होने कभी भी समाज में शान्ति भंग करने के कृत्य नहीं किये। बावजूद इसके हम देख रहे है कि जज / सोनिया / मोदी / केजरीवाल जैसे नेता और इनके अंध भगत इन संतो के व्यस्थागत ढाँचे को गिराने का समर्थन कर रहे है !!!
.
इस पूरी कवायद के नतीजे भयावह होंगे। आज या कल , इन संतो के अनुयायी या तो मिशनरीज / इस्लाम की शरण लेंगे या फिर नक्सली बन जाएंगे। किसी निशस्त्र एवं क़ानून का पालन करने वाले समूह का दमन करना आसान है , लेकिन सिर्फ तब तक जब तक कि वे लोग क़ानून का पालन करते रहने के फैसले पर टिके रहे।
.
सिर्फ तब तक जब तक कि वे लोग क़ानून का पालन करते रहने के फैसले पर टिके रहे।
.
